## `AGO-2720-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** EIS-Impedance Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Battery AC Internal Resistance, Pouch Swelling & Nail Penetration
